## Document 042: 20261116【投研報告】市場快訊_輝達產品時程恐因領導層變動遞延，台股供應鏈重挫

Report source: Yuanta (市場評論)
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 4

輝達產品時程恐因領導層變動遞延，台股供應鏈重挫
2026 年 11 月 16 日
一、市場動態
1. 輝達執行長閃辭衝擊延燒，台股重挫逾 2500 點：黃仁勳於 11 月 12 日辭去輝達總裁、
執行長及董事職務，由財務長 Colette Kress 暫代執行長，資深董事 Harvey Jones 出
任首任董事長，市場擔憂產品時程與決策速度出現不確定，加上本周財報即將發布，
加深市場觀望情緒。台股今日受上週五美股半導體重挫拖累開低走低，收最低至
41,040 點、下跌 2635 點或 6.03%。台積電跌 135 元或 5.74%，聯發科跌逾 7%，台

達電、創意及台光電、欣興等 PCB／載板族群觸及跌停，高價 AI 股成為賣壓重心。
2. AI 晶片族群同步降溫，資金由「換股」轉為「降低整體曝險」：輝達換帥後，市場原預
期資金將轉進 AMD、博通及 ASIC 等替代方案，惟上週五美國半導體族群全面回檔，
替代受惠股跌幅甚至更大，顯示投資人擔憂的已非單一公司接班問題，而是 AI 投資循
環的整體風險。
二、下跌原因
1. 領導層更替落地，產品時程與決策速度出現不確定：輝達在此事件之前，約 60 名高階
主管直接向黃仁勳匯報，權力高度集中，接班空窗期決策速度恐下降。即使在其任內，
年更節奏已觸及供應鏈物理極限，Rubin CPX 已移出路線圖，Kyber 機櫃傳延至 2028
年。新任管理層若偏重成本與執行穩定，路線圖可能改為「大世代兩年、中間推出強
化版」，800V HVDC、共同封裝光學（CPO）、 M9 等級銅箔基板等升級導入時點恐再
遞延。
2. 規格升級放緩衝擊「本夢比」，高價零組件股首當其衝：台廠 2027 年成長有相當部分
來自 Vera Rubin 單櫃價值約為 GB300 兩倍，而單櫃價值主要來自規格升級。市場擔

憂高階液冷、HVDC 電源與光互連若由 2027 年遞延至 2028 年放量，供應鏈單價、
毛利率與 EPS 成長預估恐面臨下修；缺乏技術突破支撐的高本益比族群評價修正，今
日 PCB／CCL、散熱及電源族群跌停家數最多，即反映此一疑慮。
3. 生態系融資與出口管制成為新增變數：市場估計輝達每季自由現金流約 500 億至 600
億美元，若過渡期暫緩投資及融資交易，生態系資金供給可能暫時減少 500 億美元以
上；據報 Kress 曾反對為 OpenAI 提供最高 1,050 億美元擔保，新增融資審核恐趨嚴，
依賴輝達融資的 NeoCloud 及其供應商受影響較大。此外，事件涉及晶片流向中國之
指控，美方據報擬將訂定較嚴格出口限制，市場估潛在曝險約占輝達營收一成。
4. 期中選舉後，可能的出口管制收緊以及短線獲利了結需求：此事件涉及晶片流向中國之
指控，出口管制收緊將衝擊整體 AI 晶片；台股上週反彈近 4,000 點，短線獲利了結需
求升高，避險心態未退，放大 AI 權值股賣壓。

圖一：輝達執行長更替後，可能影響路徑分析
資料來源：元大銀行整理，Bloomberg，UBS
三、市場看法
1. 2027 年出貨大致鎖定，衝擊先落在評價而非營收：Vera Rubin 已於 8 月量產出貨、
2027 年放量；輝達對供應商之採購與產能承諾達 2,790 億美元，台積電、HBM 及主
要組裝廠 2027 年產能多已預訂。廣達法說表示迄今未見客戶調整訂單，鴻海亦預期
明年 AI 機櫃出貨高雙位數成長。依 ToAlpha 基本情境推算，輝達 CoWoS 份額相對
原本路徑 2027、2028 年僅分別減少 2 與 4 個百分點，CoWoS 總量僅減約 0.6%與

0.8%，影響主要在 2028 年後的產品節奏。
表一：輝達份額變動對 AI 平台與台股供應鏈之影響（情境推估）
項目（相對原本路徑） 2027 年 2028 年
輝達占 CoWoS 需求份額 −2 個百分點 −4 個百分點
輝達平台出貨量 −4.4% −8.9%
AMD 與 ASIC 平台出貨量 +2.5% +5.8%
CoWoS 總量（台積電、封測） −0.6% −0.8%
以輝達平台為主之台股 AI 營收 −3.4% −6.7%
GPU 與 ASIC 兩邊都有之台股 AI 營收 −1.3% −2.3%
以 ASIC 為主之台股 AI 營收 +1.5% +3.6%
資料來源：Bloomberg，元大銀行整理；此為 Bloomberg 情境推估之 AI 營收影響，非盈餘預估
2. 台股供應鏈由分化轉為齊跌，ASIC 族群抗跌性消失：依曝險分組，10 月 29 日至 11
月 12 日以輝達平台為主者平均下跌 14.0%，GPU 與 ASIC 兩邊都有者下跌 17.0%，以
ASIC 為主者下跌 9.4%，同期加權指數下跌 7.1%。兩邊都有之族群多為前波漲幅大的

規格升級零組件股，跌幅反而最深，顯示市場賣的是「評價」而非「訂單」；今日台股
盤勢延續上周五費半情緒，以 ASIC 為主及兩邊都有之代表股平均重挫逾 8%，以輝達
平台為主者跌約 4.4%，顯示賣壓轉向前波漲多、評價較高的 ASIC 與規格升級股，市
場已由「區分受益者」轉為「全面降低 AI 曝險」，短線錯殺機會浮現。

3. 算力多元化早於本次事件，Google TPU 與 AMD 最直接受惠：根據彭博在本次事件發
生前的統計，市場預估輝達占全球 CoWoS 需求比重將由 2026 年 56%降至 2027 年
45%，AMD 由 9%升至 20%，聯發科由 3%升至 7%，預計本次事件發生後，此趨勢
可能進一步加劇。由於 AMD、博通與雲端業者自研 ASIC 同樣高度依賴台積電先進製
程及台廠組裝與零組件，訂單多在台灣供應鏈內部移轉，台灣在 AMD、Google 供應
鏈同樣扮演重要角色。
表三：全球 CoWoS 需求之客戶比例
年度 CoWoS 需求（千片） 輝達 AMD 博通（Google TPU 等） 聯發科
2024 372 54% 11% 18% —
2025 689 62% 9% 12% —
2026（估） 1,394 56% 9% 22% 3%

2027（估） 2,694 45% 20% 18% 7%
資料來源：元大銀行整理，Bloomberg；2026、2027 年為估計值
4. 歷史經驗顯示 CEO 更替後股價無一致方向，基本面才是中期關鍵：回顧美股大型科技
公司交棒案例，計畫性交棒如 Apple、Microsoft 後 12 個月股價分別上漲 77.1%與
20.1%，但同為規劃性交棒的 Amazon 卻下跌 35.3%；突發撤換的 Intel 兩案，3 個月
內均下跌逾 9%，12 個月後則轉為正報酬。惟過去歷史經驗僅能作為參考，輝達後續
走勢仍取決於事件發展與基本面變化而定。
表四：美股大型科技公司 CEO 更替後股價表現（%）

資料來源：Bloomberg、公司資料、元大銀行整理；黃底為突發性更替案例
5. 本週三大事件為短線關鍵驗證點：（一）輝達將於美東 11 月 17 日（台灣時間 11 月 18
日凌晨）公布第三季財報，為首度由暫代執行長主持之法說會，焦點在 2028 會計年度
約七成成長指引是否重申、Vera Rubin 放量節奏及新增融資態度；（二）Anthropic 預
計 11 月 18 日定價上市，為檢驗 AI 市場熱度之指標；（三）黃仁勳預計感恩節前赴國
會作證，牽動晶片出口管制法案走向。若財報與指引穩健，市場有望由情緒面回歸基本

面。
四、投資建議
1. 科技股短期審慎、中期仍正向，AI 供應鏈基本面持續具支撐：黃仁勳辭任使輝達面臨
管理層接班、企業治理及政策調查等不確定性，加上 Fed 升息預期及 AI 資料中心監
管疑慮，市場短線波動可能延續。然而，該事件並未直接改變 AI 算力需求，且上週
CoreWeave Q3 財報營收年增 168%、台積電 10 月營收年增 59.7%，均顯示 AI 基礎
建設投資仍具動能。整體對科技股維持短期審慎、但中長期基本面仍在，台灣及日本
等具技術競爭優勢、獲利與現金流穩健的半導體及 AI 供應鏈亦值得留意。惟市場焦點
已由資本支出規模轉向投資報酬與現金流，後續仍須關注輝達財報及管理團隊能否維
持客戶信心。
2. 以股債平衡穿越波動，善用多元資產降低單一事件風險：台股權重高度集中於台積電
與 AI 供應鏈，對單一事件敏感度高。建議將集中度過高的台股科技部位，適度轉向全
球型或多重資產商品，搭配中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債穩定收益；待接班人
選、財報指引與出口管制方向明朗後，再逐步提高風險資產比重。

圖二：台灣加權指數近一年走勢
資料來源：Bloomberg

市場焦點
期中選舉
• 美國期中選舉後，市場擔憂民主黨可能放緩AI資料中心的建設並提高對AI的監管，促使科技股
面臨估值修正壓力。但從基本面觀察，2026年標普500企業獲利年增率預估已由7月的26.5%
大幅上修至36.1%，其中資訊科技產業預估成長64.5%，通訊服務亦有52.0%。大型科技企業
仍具備穩健現金流與持續投入AI基礎建設的能力，AI長期發展趨勢尚未改變，產業基本面不宜
因短期估值修正而過度看淡。
中國A股
• 短期來看A股反彈行情有機會延續，主因為美國期中選舉落幕、中國政策環境偏寬鬆，以及A
股估值與情緒指標落在歷史相對低檔，具備超跌反彈條件，政策面方面，人行可能進一步降息，
政策利率由1.25%下調至1%，搭配IPO超募資金回購、穩定資金加大ETF買入力道，有助於改

善市場流動性並提振投資信心。
石油/黃金
• 波斯灣國家能源出口替代方案已略見成效，不過由於替代路徑運輸成本較高，能源價格仍居
高不下，觀察過去幾週出口及價格變化，推測在美伊關係未有明確變化的情境下，國際油價
將在每桶100美元附近震盪。
• 央行購金需求維持高檔，但相較去年同期累計未進一步放大。在此情境下，短中期黃金價格
能否進一步突破的關鍵，可能取決於聯準會貨幣政策的調整空間。

• 選民不滿情緒發酵，民主黨在期中選舉取得重大突破：伊朗局勢久拖不決、通膨持續攀升，削弱選民對川普政府的支持，民
主黨因此在期中選舉中於參、眾兩院取得過半席次，新一屆國會將在2027年1月3日就職，進入白宮與國會分由不同政黨掌
控的「分裂政府」時代。州長選戰方面，民主黨也成功翻轉多個原由共和黨長期執政的州，州長席次占比睽違20年再度超越
共和黨，其中德州、喬治亞州、俄亥俄州及愛荷華州的選情翻轉尤其受到矚目。
• 國會制衡與州政府版圖變化，政策走向仍存不確定性： 儘管民主黨成功奪回眾議院，但在參議院尚未取得足以單獨推動重大
法案的關鍵席次，例如除了財政預算相關法案可以用預算調節程序簡單多數通過外，一般的法案都需要60票才能通過，因此
未來重大政策仍須仰賴跨黨協商，市場對政策劇烈轉向的疑慮可望有所緩解。不過，民主黨掌握更多州政府，也可能促使部

分州在川普剩餘兩年任期內，進一步制衡或抵制聯邦施政議程，並對2028年總統大選的選務布局產生重要影響。

• 美國經濟具韌性，紮根美股未來底氣：美國期中選舉後，市場擔憂民主黨可能放
緩AI資料中心的建設並提高對AI的監管，促使科技股面臨估值修正壓力。但從基

鏈；然而，大型科技企業仍具備穩健現金流與持續投入AI基礎建設的能力，AI長
期發展趨勢尚未改變，產業基本面不宜因短期估值修正而過度看淡。

美國政治藍色浪潮來襲：AI數據中心建設受阻與產業鏈資產重估
• 政治監管與地方風向轉變：AI基礎設施擴建面臨停工與審查的壓力，民主黨在多個關鍵州分贏得州長選舉，並伴隨地方公投
限制措施的升溫，使得原本蓬勃發展的AI數據中心建設計畫大幅放緩。
• 關鍵州分政策轉嚴：德州、喬治亞州與俄亥俄州等AI數據中心投資重鎮，當選民主黨州長普遍對數據中心開發採取強硬立場。
德州喊出暫停新建並取消稅收優惠，喬治亞州提出全州暫緩新項目，俄亥俄州與威斯康辛州亦對能源消耗、環境審查及稅收
補貼提出嚴格管制條件。
• 地方公投與審查週期拉長：全美46項涉及數據中心的地方限制措施中，高達40項趨向收緊或已通過更嚴格限制，未來開發
項目將面臨更長審批週期、更嚴格電網接入審查及更高公用事業與監管合規成本。

• 未成熟開發商延期風險高：以全美儲備規模最大的德州為例，超過半數項目尚處於早期階段，且大量由缺乏營運經驗的初次
開發商主導。在監管與水電資源收緊下，此類項目極易面臨延期或取消的風險。
AI建設熱潮面臨民主黨藍色浪潮衝擊 46項地方數據中心措施投票結果分布
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.11.05

美國製造業景氣加速擴張，原物料成本壓力仍牽制通膨降溫
• 製造業擴張動能增強，生產與新訂單同步改善：美國10月ISM製造業PMI升至55.5，較9月54.5增加1%，連續第10個月
維持擴張；其中生產指數升至59.0，創2021年11月以來新高，新訂單指數亦升至55.5，顯示製造業接單及生產活動持
續增溫，且存貨指數回升至51.4，反映企業庫存由收縮轉為擴張，製造業景氣復甦動能進一步增強。
• 需求回升伴隨成本壓力，通膨降溫仍面臨挑戰：供應商交貨指數升至59.1，代表交貨速度持續放緩，連續11個月呈現相
同趨勢；客戶端庫存指數維持41.6，顯示庫存水位仍偏低，有利後續補庫存需求。然而，原物料價格指數雖由77.9降至
75.2，仍連續第25個月處於上漲區間，反映鋼鋁、石化及進口商品成本壓力尚未消退，可能使通膨降溫進程更加緩慢。

• 後市觀察：美國製造業景氣持續擴張，反映企業需求及經濟活動仍具韌性，有助支撐企業營收與獲利；惟原物料價格維
持高檔，可能增加通膨黏著性，使聯準會貨幣政策維持審慎，並對美債殖利率形成支撐。後續市場將關注製造業需求能
否延續，以及成本壓力是否逐步傳導至終端物價。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.11.3
今年以來ISM製造業PMI趨勢
資料來源：ISM Manufacturing PMI Report， 2026.11.3

企業財務與行業分化：高槓桿新雲業者受創，巨頭發揮定價優勢
• 政策與審查趨嚴使得整體AI建設成本上揚，但在產業內卻呈現出極端的強者恆強、弱者面臨淘汰的兩極趨勢。
• 新雲公司(Neoclouds)面臨流動性與違約風險：若數據中心建設延誤或無法如期接入電網，收入推遲產生，而這類新興雲端
服務商普遍具高財務槓桿，高額利息費用持續累積，導致現金流期限錯配，將提高其再融資風險、擴大信用利差，甚至觸發
合約中的不可抗力條款與債務違約風險。
• 超大規模雲端巨頭(Hyperscalers)享受供應趨緊紅利：對科技巨頭而言，基礎設施擴建受阻反而是利多。已擁有現成數據中
心與電力保障的巨頭定價權大幅提升，算力供給緊俏能將雲端計算服務價格維持在高檔。此外，巨頭有能力自行籌資進行專
用發電或直接與公用事業公司談判，進一步拉開與新進入者的差距。

• AI六大支柱亮警報：支撐AI熱潮持續發展六大條件(訂單積壓、融資、算力需求、競爭、政治、聯準會)中，政治、競爭及聯
準會三項已現疲軟，特別是詞元(Token)成本持續下降、模型可替代性提高，低價模型加劇競爭，持續擠壓軟體獲利空間。
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.11.05
詞元(Token)成本持續下降• 小結與投資建議：期中選舉引發的政策變革，正將AI
行業從無差別大舉擴建帶入嚴格監管與高成本的新階
段。供給側瓶頸(電網、土地、監管)將淘汰財務體質孱
弱、高度依賴外部融資的高槓桿新興開發商；供給受
限也鞏固已具備規模與資源保障的雲端巨頭壟斷優勢
與定價權。 投資建議避開高槓桿新雲與中小型開發商，
聚焦資本雄厚且自備水電資源的超大規模雲端巨頭，
密切關注聯準會貨幣政策與信用利差變化，AI行業的
金融條件正在收緊，若聯準會降息放慢或信用利差持
續擴大，AI供應鏈中的高估值軟體與硬體股可能面臨
新一輪估值修正。

AI資本支出持續攀升，現金流承壓加劇企業信用分化
• AI基礎建設投資擴大，企業融資需求同步升溫：AI競賽已由
模型及技術開發延伸至資料中心、運算晶片與電力基礎設施
建置，帶動大型科技企業持續提高資本支出。Microsoft、
Alphabet、Amazon等企業近期公布的財報均顯示，AI相關
投資已進入高強度建置階段，市場預估大型科技企業2026年
AI基礎建設投資將超過7,000億美元，龐大資金需求也使市場
更加關注企業融資成本、資本配置效率及未來現金流表現。
• 財報反映投資成本，現金流與信用評等成市場檢驗重點：Q3
財報顯示，Alphabet及Amazon均因AI基礎建設投資大幅增
加，使自由現金流承受壓力；其中Amazon近12個月自由現
金流轉為負值，Alphabet單季亦出現負自由現金流。相較之

下，Microsoft仍維持正向自由現金流，反映企業財務承受能
力有所差異。此外，Meta因AI資本支出規模擴大及未來現金
流不確定性，遭標普全球調降信用評等展望至負向，顯示市
場已開始將AI投資效益納入企業信用風險評估。
• 市場看法：AI長期需求仍具成長潛力，但市場評價將逐步由
資本支出規模轉向投資報酬率、自由現金流及財務槓桿管理。
而具備穩定營運現金流、較高資本回收效率及明確AI商業化
成果的企業，較有能力維持投資動能；反之，若資本支出持
續攀升而營收及獲利未能同步跟上，將增加企業融資需求與
信用風險，並使科技企業間的評價差異進一步擴大。
大型科技企業擴大資本支出，承諾規模持續攀升
AI產業表外承諾大幅揚升
資料來源：Morgan Stanley Research，企業10-Q、10-K、8-K申報文件，ZeroHedge，
兆豐銀行彙整。

A股短期反彈行情可望延續，長期聚焦科技成長
• A股短期反彈行情可望延續，長期聚焦科技成長：從A股內部條件來看，
市場經歷長時間修正後，估值與情緒指標已降至歷史低檔，創業板本
益比回落至約26.5%，全市場站上200日均線個股占比僅約一成，顯示
悲觀情緒已相當充分反映，搭配市場成交熱度回升，盤面觀察近期銀
行、消費等權值板塊逐漸止穩，科技成長類股也開始出現籌碼與評價
修復，顯示市場輪動條件逐步成熟。短期而言，A股反彈行情仍有機會
延續，主因為美國期中選舉落幕、中國政策環境偏寬鬆，以及A股估值
與情緒指標落在歷史相對低檔，具備超跌反彈條件，政策面方面，人
行可能進一步降息，政策利率由1.25%下調至1%，搭配IPO超募資金
回購、穩定資金加大ETF買入力道，有助於改善市場流動性並提振投資

信心。
• 投資建議：操作上可優先布局半導體、AI硬體、通訊設備、電子零組
件、醫藥及機器人等具成長題材的族群，並留意商業航太等新興應用，
不過，反彈過程仍須觀察政策落實程度、企業獲利修復及海外市場變
化，中長期而言，A股仍以科技創新與科技服務實體經濟為核心主軸，
相關政策方向並未改變，建議維持科技成長為核心配置，搭配具景氣
復甦潛力的週期與消費產業，採取分批布局。
各指數估值與情緒指標已降至歷史低檔

中國10月製造業PMI跌回收縮區間，產需動能同步降溫
• 中國10月製造業PMI跌回收縮區間，產需動能同步降溫：中國10月製造業PMI降至49.3%，低於市場預期，也跌破50榮枯線，
顯示製造業景氣再度轉弱，主要受到十一長假前需求提前釋放，以及前期海外聖誕訂單集中出貨後進入調整期影響，使需求與
生產動能同步放緩，新訂單指數和生產指數皆下降，顯示企業生產活動亦有所收斂，不過若與去年同期相比，新訂單與生產指
數分別增加0.6及0.1個百分點，顯示製造業基本面仍具一定韌性，景氣表現優於去年同期，此外企業對未來仍維持審慎樂觀態
度，生產經營活動預期指數為53.4%，雖較前月略降，但仍位於擴張區間。
• 展望後市：隨著專項債加速投放，及8,000億元政策性金融工具逐步轉化為實體投資，基建及製造業需求有望持續釋放，帶動裝

備製造、鋼鐵等產業鏈景氣改善，此外11月為企業衝刺全年營運目標的重要時點，訂單接單與生產活動將較10月回溫，產業表
現方面，科技業因受惠於政策扶持及產業升級趨勢，維持強勁動能，消費品製造業則有望受雙十一購物帶動成長。

民主黨主張監管政策 間接支撐黃金價格表現
• 期中選舉為黃金價格帶來短期支撐：民主黨主導國會加深兩黨政治僵局的擔憂，加上可能推動更嚴格的AI產業監管，市場擔
憂對美國經濟成長帶來負面影響，進而增加聯準會採取寬鬆貨幣政策的可能性，同時美元走貶也是上週支撐金價的因素之一。
• 央行需求基本面穩健：世界黃金協會最新報告指出，各國央行在9月持續增加黃金持有量，當月淨購買量達61噸，為今年迄
今最高的單月總量。同時數據顯示，全球各國央行所申報的黃金淨購買量為228噸，較去年同期下降 7%。
• 觀點：央行購金需求維持高檔，但相較去年同期累計未進一步放大，同時高水準的美債殖利率排擠黃金的避險需求。在此情
境下，短中期黃金價格能否進一步突破的關鍵，可能取決於聯準會貨幣政策的調整空間。雖然市場大致排除激進升息的預期，

但緩步升息仍是基本情境，預料近期黃金價格將在每盎司4,000~4,200震盪。消息面來看，美伊衝突維持僵局，不過若是荷
姆茲海峽議題能有重大突破，將為黃金帶來進一步上漲空間。
資料來源：世界黃金協會，資料統計至9月底，2026.11.4。
金價短線技術面壓力在4,200美元(週線)

期中選舉牽動，上週國際油價大幅震盪
• 選舉結果令國際油價大跌：選舉結果出爐後，市場預期民主黨將在預算與軍事決策上牽制總統川普，促使中東僵局加速透過
外交協商落幕。結果公布當天布蘭特油價單日大跌約9%，至每桶94美元，臨近週末則小幅反彈。上週油價整體收跌約16%，
主要反映選舉風險溢價消退，不過美伊僵局未變，油價仍在100美元附近震盪。
• 中東原油出口接近戰前水準，但成品油出口仍大幅落後：波斯灣國家正致力原油出口替代方案，目標擺脫荷姆茲海峽航運依
賴。石油出口量近幾週雖有所回落，但已接近戰前平均水準；然而，成品油出口動能恢復緩慢，目前仍遠不及戰前平均，這
也是造成全球能源通膨的主要原因。
• 觀點：波斯灣國家能源出口替代方案已略見成效，不過由於替代路徑運輸成本較高，能源價格仍居高不下，觀察過去幾週出

口及價格變化，推測在美伊關係未有明確變化的情境下，國際油價將在每桶100美元附近震盪。

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。
警 語

### Page 1

King Slide

Buy: strong 3Q26 results, 88.2% GM; solid 
’27/28E sales/margin; cut PO to NT$12,988

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 12,988 TWD | Price: 8,165 TWD

3Q26 results: another impressive beat with 88.2% GM 
King Slide’s 3Q26 GM/OPM hit record highs of 88.2%/82.9% for the fourth/second 
consecutive quarter, respectively, well above BofAe of 83.7%/78.2% and consensus of 
84.0%/78.6%. OP (+62% QoQ, +383% YoY) was 7%/5% above BofAe/consensus. We 
attribute the solid margins/results to sizable sales scale (+60% QoQ, +297% YoY) and 
better operating efficiency. With non-operating gain (mainly net interest income), NP 
(+65% QoQ, +266% YoY) was 8%/7% above BofAe/consensus (Exhibit 3). 
BofAe 4Q26 sales -20% QoQ; solid 2027/28 sales/margin 
We now expect King Slide’s 4Q26 sales to decline 20% QoQ, from the high 3Q26 base 
and amid GPU platform transition. We believe Sep/Oct sales (-31%/+2% MoM) shall 
serve as evidence for slower order momentum. Our 4Q26 sales forecast implies flat-to-
slightly higher MoM sales in Nov/Dec. We now expect another 45%/20% YoY sales 
growth in 2027/28, with stronger pull-in demand resuming in 1Q27. In our view, 
2027/28 sales should be supported by various AI projects across platforms (GPU/ASIC/ 
AMD) and clients (global/regional CSPs, neoclouds, AI start-ups, enterprises). We also 
keep monitoring the capacity ramp-up from the new US Houston site, which may bring 
further sales upside. We expect high and sustainable GM at 85.0% both in 2027/28. We 
see FX as a potential swing factor to GM, given the company’s natural-hedging policy.  
Trim PO to NT$12,988: lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy 
We lower our 2026-27E EPS by 0-4% but lift our 2028E EPS by 7%, mainly to reflect 
3Q26 beat and stronger margins. Yet, we cut PO to NT$12,988 (28x 2027E EPS) from 
NT$14,888 (32x 2027E EPS), with valuation base unchanged but a lower P/E multiple. 
We now use 28x (2SD above average since IPO) on valuation de-rating across AI supply 
chain amid macro uncertainty. Nevertheless, we reiterate Buy on King Slide in view of its 
lion share in AI server rail market, diversified client/project base, strong margin profile.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
King Slide is the global top server rail kit manufacturer from 
Taiwan. It also supplies slide rail kit for kitchen/furniture. Its 
key server clients include global cloud service providers and 
enterprise servers.

King Slide was founded in 1986 and was publicly listed on 
Taiwan Stock Exchange on 25 June 2008. Its headquarter 
and main production sites are located in Kaohsiung 
(Taiwan). It also has capacity in Houston (US).

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to King Slide given its leading 
position in server rail to ride on strong AI market demand 
with rising content value thanks to increasing component 
density and customization. We highlight King Slide's early 
step into server market since 2001 with proven track 
record, much larger scale, time-to-market capability, higher 
automation level, and 3,600+ patents as strong moats for 
high/above-peer margins.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
King Slide (KGSLF) 
Our PO of NT$12,988 is based on 28x 2027E EPS. We use 28x P/E multiple (2SD above 
P/E average since listing) in view of strong AI server market demand, leading position in 
server rail, solid/high margin profiles, and double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28E. Its 
early step into server market since 2001 with proven track record, much larger scale 
than peer, and 3,600+ patents as strong moats for high/above-peer margins.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) even stronger AI server demand, (2) margin upside thanks 
to larger scale and/or better efficiency, (3) better kitchen slides market demand, (4) 
faster capacity expansion, (5) favorable FX movement (mainly USD/NTD) for GM and/or 
non-operating items.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand due to GPU/components 
shortage, (2) GM pressure due to higher raw material cost (e.g. steel, aluminum), (3) 
softer kitchen slides market demand under macro uncertainty/weakness, (4) delayed 
product certification, (5) slower capacity expansion, (6) unfavorable FX movement 
(mainly USD/NTD) to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

 Measures Definitions 
Business Performance Numerator Denominator 
Return On Capital Employed NOPAT = (EBIT + Interest Income) × (1 − Tax Rate) + Goodwill Amortization Total Assets − Current Liabilities + ST Debt + Accumulated Goodwill 
Amortization 
Return On Equity Net Income Shareholders’ Equity 
Operating Margin Operating Profit Sales 
Earnings Growth Expected 5 Year CAGR From Latest Actual N/A 
Free Cash Flow Cash Flow From Operations − Total Capex N/A  
Quality of Earnings Numerator Denominator 
Cash Realization Ratio Cash Flow From Operations Net Income 
Asset Replacement Ratio Capex Depreciation 
Tax Rate Tax Charge Pre-Tax Income

Positive pricing signals for Yageo: Chaozhou Three-Circle (300408.SZ/6951.HK,
not covered) spoke at our Asia Pacific Summit. It said that its 3Q26 MLCC ASPs rose
20–30% q/q on catch-up increases in mid/low-capacitance products. We see this as

supportive of firmer standard MLCC pricing at Yageo.
Tightness extends into 1H27: Three-Circle said its utilization rate was at 100% and
it expects high-capacitance shortages through 1H27. Management said Murata/
SEMCO’s capacity shifts were happening and this would continue to tighten
standard MLCC supply, supporting our view on Yageo (rated Overweight) and its
utilization and pricing.
Technology limits competitive overlap: Three-Circle said its  AI mix was single-digit,

with small, high-capacitance MLCCs still a capability gap. This suggests limited
immediate competition for the more demanding products within Yageo’s high-
capacitance portfolio.
Three-Circle said its >40% CY26 capacity growth outlook implies >70B MLCC
units/month by year-end, versus ~50B at the start of the year. This compares to
our estimate on Yageo at ~80B/month, although we don't know the exact
specifications used by either company, so that could limit direct capacity

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

ab 23 November 2026
Global Research
Initiation of Coverage
Elite Material
Manifesting AI-driven upcycle; initiate at Buy
Initiate on TW CCL - EMC: major beneficiary of  structural CCL material upgrade
We initiate EMC with a Buy rating and PT NT$5,000. The company is a global CCL leader
that enables AI systems with high-speed and low-loss materials. We see booming GPU/
ASIC demand driving not just CCL consumption but also a shift toward higher-grade

CCL products to meet high-speed and signal integrity requirements. M8 CCL has
become standard for Blackwell and will migrate to M9/M10 for Feynman.  We anticipate
these structural upgrades and capacity expansion to support  62% sales CAGR   through
'26-'28E; and anticipate product mix shift toward  M7+ to drive margin expansion.
Resilient fundamentals underpinned by strong positioning
Our industry studies suggest EMC is well positioned across AI GPU/ASIC projects. It was

the dominant supplier for Nvidia Hopper OAM/UBB boards. Doosan has begun to supply
compute tray from Blackwell, while EMC and SYTECH supplies the switch tray. We
believe EMC is also one of the major suppliers for Google TPU (splitting with Panasonic)
and AWS Trainium (other suppliers including a TW CCL supplier with potential new
entrants such as SYTECH and Nan Ya Plastics). We see AI platforms ramping up
production in H226 that are enlarging the supply/demand imbalance, driving further

CCL pricing uplifts. We believe EMC's capacity scale and major projects' share allocation
backed by its upstream sourcing will support its growth realization of strong AI demand.
Investing in M8+ for future growth with margin expansion
As a major supplier for  GPU and ASIC platforms, EMC is expanding capacity to capture
future growth. The company announced an investment total of NT$98.6bn through
2027-2030F to add capacity across Taiwan, Mainland China, and Malaysia, where its

total capacity will increase from 5,850k sheets/month in 2026F to 15,250k sheets/
month by 2030F, and these additions would be supporting M8+ CCL demand. M7+
increased from 55–60% of product mix in Q126 to 65–70% in Q326. Looking ahead,
management guides for M7+ to reach 70–80%  in 2027F as AI server specs continue to
move up the material ladder. We expect earnings to grow at 82% CAGR through 2026-
2028E, outpacing topline growth of 62% for the same periods, supported by favourable

product mix shift. Meanwhile, EMC is attempting to build ABF substrate CCL capacity
and going through qualification in H226. We believe high-end CCL remains its key focus
and note that these capacity additions are fungible for M8/M9 high-speed CCL
production, and we have not yet factored in ABF substrate CCL business potential.
Valuation: Initiate at Buy with PT$5,000
In light of market concerns on AI slowdown, we note that our estimates are below

consensus factoring in slower capacity ramp (10-30% lower than announced plans).
Our NT$5,000 PT is based on 12x 2028E PE, which we think is conservative at the low-
end of its  historical trading range of 7-20x. Despite more conservative estimates, still see
attractive risk-reward back by  37%'26-'30E earnings CAGR, implying only 0.3x PEG.

Elite Material UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Is favourable pricing environment and current CCL industry upcycle sustainable?
We continue to see AI CCL content value increases as computing and networking/interconnect
demands surges. Extended equipment lead time and qualification process along with increasing
manufacturing challenges are also suggesting more constrained upstream inputs. We expect

persisting CCL industry shortage to drive better pricing power and believe high-end CCL have
entered a spec-driven structural upgrade cycle, not a temporary commodity uptick.
Q: Could high-end CCL drive the next phase of growth for Elite Material?
Yes. Among Taiwanese suppliers, we believe EMC is best positioned to capitalise on rapid AI
deployment based on its competitive high-end material offerings across GPU/ASIC platforms. We
expect EMC will continue to benefit from growing AI-driven computing/networking demand and

high-end CCL spec upgrades as a key supplier for major GPU and several US cloud service providers'
ASIC CCL materials. We are positive on its solid business fundamentals and see its business scale,
customer exposure, further pricing uplifts, favourable product mix shift could drive strong earnings
expansion in the next few years.
Among CCL supply chain, we believe EMC is best positioned for further re-rating given its leading
position in high-end CCL materials with capacity scale, high-end product offerings, and sourcing

capability. The company has board customer exposure among major GPU and ASIC platforms, best
positioned to capitalize AI-driven spec upgrades.
Top 6 CSP capex spending has revised up another leg, strengthening visibility into AI-related CCL
demand. Hyperscaler capex grew 25% QoQ/ 101% YoY in Q326 to US$252.7bn, with UBS 2026/27E
forecasts revised up 2%/18% to imply +96%/+74% YoY growth to US$897bn/US$1.6tn.
Meanwhile, the pricing backdrop remains supportive,   with Taiwanese CCL suppliers raising prices by

10-30% QoQ in Q126 and Q226, followed by further double-digit increases in August and
September. EMC’s latest commentary reinforces this trend,  noting 5-20% price increases across its
portfolio in Q426F and further upside possible should the supply-demand mismatch persist.
EMC’s share price has fallen 56% from its Aug peak (vs. TAIEX -21%), amid the broader tech sell-off
and rising concerns over slower AI spending. The stock now trades at 10x/7x our 2027/2028E P/E,

below its long-term historical avg. of 13.5x and the lower-end of the  AI cycle 10-48x, despite our
'27/'28 EPS estimates already  c.4%/4% below consensus. We believe current valuation increasingly
prices in slower AI demand, while giving insufficient credit to EMC’s technology leadership and
continued M8/M9/M10 upgrades, which we believe warrant a premium to CCL peers. Reflecting
recent market sentiment, we conservatively value EMC at  12x 2028E P/E, around its historical

median. This remains attractive given 37% 2026-30E earnings CAGR and implies  0.3x PEG.

Elite Material  was founded in Taoyuan in 1992 and started as a conventional FR-4 copper clad thin
core laminate and prepreg manufacturer. Over time, EMC has established its global presence as the

world's largest supplier of halogen-free high-speed CCLs with its leading technology expertise. In
2025, infrastructure applications, including servers, switches, and LEO satellite accounted for 65-
70% of revenue, followed by mobile devices (20-25%), and automation/industrial/others (5-10%).

Company Description

### Page 3

Elite Material UBS Research
business overview
Founded in 1992, EMC  started as a manufacturer of conventional FR-4 CCL and
prepregs used in printed circuit boards. The company began moving up the value chain
with its entry into HDI in 1997, followed by  the introduction of halogen-free materials
licensed from Hitachi Chemical in 2002, allowing EMC to establish a leading position in
handset HDI applications. Smartphones subsequently became EMC's core franchise with

the company sharing c70% market share and becoming a key supplier to Apple as well
as leading Android brands.
EMC began its next major product transition in 2017, when it entered the high-speed
digital materials market with ~0% market share. Over the following seven years, the
company expanded its low-loss material portfolio and customer qualifications across
high-speed switches, general servers and cloud infrastructure, before benefiting from
the rapid growth of AI servers and CSP ASIC platforms. EMC’s high-speed digital market

share reached 28% in 2023, when it became the global No.1 supplier, and increased
further to 38.6% in 2025. This transition has materially reshaped its revenue mix:
infrastructure increased from around 30% of revenue in 2019-21 to 75-80% currently,
while handset exposure declined from 50% to 10-15%. EMC’s customer base has
consequently broadened from handset-centric exposure toward leading networking
and cloud customers, including NVIDIA-related AI platforms and major CSP ASIC

programs. Building on its HDI expertise, EMC further expanded into IC substrate
materials in 2020, and has grown its market share to 1.6% by 2025 where it is now the
only non-Japanese/Korean player with substrate material technology expertise.

HDI leadership underpins EMC’s longstanding consumer-tech franchise
EMC has historically been the global leader in mobile-device applications, with 70%

market share in mobile-phone CCL and over 80% in 5G smartphone CCL. Mobile
devices remain a meaningful 10-15% of EMC's Q326 sales, supported by  its
longstanding leadership in HDI materials, where it has ranked No.1 globally for more
than 20 years with over 70% share. EMC began developing conventional HDI in 1997,
followed by Anylayer HDI in 2010 and mSAP in 2017, progressively enabling finer lines
and higher circuit density. Unlike high-layer-count (HLC) boards, which primarily add

layers to accommodate more circuitry, HDI uses microvias, finer routing and sequential
lamination to pack more interconnects into a smaller board area, making it particularly
well suited to smartphones, tablets and notebooks where miniaturization is critical. The
repeated lamination and finer structures also place greater demands on CCL, including
dimensional stability, resin-flow control, low CTE, CAF resistance and adhesion, creating
meaningful materials know-how rather than simply a different PCB process. This

expertise helped EMC establish an early position in the Apple supply chain, when
handheld devices rose to  50% of sales in 2016.  EMC’s  historical smartphone customer
base has included Apple, Samsung, Huawei, HTC and Nokia. While consumer tech is no
longer EMC's growth focus, we view it as a stable and strategically important revenue
base, complementing the companies rapidly expanding infrastructure focus.

### Page 4

Figure 3: EMC's technology advancements
Source: Company data, courtesy of EMC
Riding the AI-driven CCL upgrade cycle, with M9 extending high-end leadership
AI infrastructure has become EMC’s core growth engine, with infrastructure reaching
roughly 75-80% of revenue in Q326 and growing 183% YoY, driven primarily by
accelerating AI server and high-speed networking demand. EMC has translated this
demand into a leading position in high-end materials, capturing 38.6% of the global

high-speed halogen-free CCL market in 2025, which should enable the company to
continue outgrowing the broader market through both share gains and content
expansion. Industry forecasts cited by the company call for high-end CCL market to
grow at a 40% CAGR in 2024-2027F and AI CCL at a 178% CAGR in 2025-2027F, while
UBS expects hyperscaler capex to grow +96%/+74% YoY in 2026/27E. As AI spending
broadens across both  GPUs and ASICs, we expect EMC to benefit from rising system

volumes as well as increasing CCL content and value per system.
The latter is driven by a structural migration toward higher-grade, lower-loss materials as
data rates and PCB complexity increase. M7-and-above extreme/super low-loss products
already account for 65-70% of EMC's revenue in Q326, with management expecting
the mix to reach over 70% in 2027. Based on our industry checks, the economic value
creation is non-linear across the material stack. While M7 typically carries a 15-50% ASP

premium over M6, M8 pricing is roughly twice that of M7. The largest step-up occurs at
M9, where ASPs are approximately 5-6x those of M6 and GMs exceed 50% versus
roughly 25-30% for M6 and over 30% for M7. Consequently, EMC's earnings sensitivity
to high-end mix expansion is substantially greater than revenue growth alone would
imply.  M9 represents the next leg of this upgrade cycle. EMC was the first supplier to
complete customer qualification from NVIDIA and expects volume shipments to begin in

H226. Management expects M9 to contribute a mid- to high-single-digit percentage of
revenue in H226 before reaching double digits in 2027. EMC is still the first source for
M9 grade applications  including selected NVIDIA Rubin  boards and next-generation
hyperscaler ASIC platforms, the competitors include potential second source Shengyi
and ITEQ who have also passed qualifications.
Nvidia: sustaining a strong position across successive platform upgrades. EMC’s

position in AI CCL was established in 2023, when it became the primary supplier of high-
speed CCL for Nvidia’s H100 GPU server boards. We believe EMC’s prior track record in
high-end HDI helped position it well as GPU boards moved toward greater layer counts,
multiple lamination and more demanding signal-integrity requirements. Nvidia's
architecture has evolved since then and the supplier landscape has become more
differentiated by board. For GB200/GB300, Doosan is the primary compute-tray CCL

supplier, while EMC is the main supplier for the switch tray, alongside Shengyi. Moving
into Vera Rubin, EMC is positioned for CPX/midplane and switch-tray applications, while
Doosan remains dominant in the compute tray. We believe its over 50% share in
NVIDIA's server CCL should remain stable as it benefits from the annual cadence
upgrades.

### Page 5

ASIC: broad hyperscaler exposure reduces reliance on Nvidia. EMC holds roughly
70% share of ASIC-server CCL, supported by exposure across leading hyperscaler
programs. Beyond Nvidia, EMC is benefiting from the continued ramp of custom silicon
at AWS, Google and Meta, alongside a migration to higher-specification materials from
M7 to M8/M8+. Key growth drivers include AWS Trainium 3, Google TPU v8 shipments
from H226, and expanding deployments of Meta's in-house accelerators. With multiple

custom-silicon platforms scaling concurrently, we expect ASIC demand to remain a
meaningful contributor through 2027, further broadening EMC's participation in
hyperscaler AI spending.
Switch: 800G/1.6T migration drives multi-year upgrade cycle
Switch-related materials account for roughly 15-20% of EMC’s infrastructure revenue.
As the connectivity backbone of AI clusters and cloud data centers, switches are seeing
rapidly rising bandwidth requirements, driving the transition from 400G toward 800G

and ultimately 1.6T. 400G remains the largest contributor today, while 800G began
ramping in 2025 and is expected to take around three years to become mainstream, and
1.6T adoption should begin in 2H26 on a similar trajectory. We expect these overlapping
upgrade cycles to support both volume growth and richer material content over the next
several years.
LEO satellite adds a niche but scalable high-speed CCL opportunity
LEO satellite has developed into a meaningful niche within EMC’s infrastructure

portfolio, accounting for roughly 6% of 2025 revenue, providing  materials across
satellite equipment, ground receiving stations, and user terminal equipment. EMC
began targeting the segment in 2023 and entered mass production for LEO satellite
materials in 2024, alongside completion of next-generation product qualification. The
opportunity fits well with EMC’s existing strengths in high-speed, low-loss laminates
and HDI, as satellite boards require strong signal integrity, thermal reliability and

compact, high-density designs.  Demand is supported by continued constellation
deployment from two major US customers, while material specifications are also
upgrading from M6 to M7 as transmission speeds increase, providing additional CCL
content per satellite. Key competitors include US-based Isola which has an established
presence in aerospace and satellite-grade high-performance laminates. We  see LEO as a
relatively small but differentiated growth driver that broadens EMC’s infrastructure

exposure beyond AI servers and switches.
Substrates represent an emerging growth leg with significant share gain
potential
Substrates remain a relatively small part of Elite Material's business today, but the
company has steadily expanded its market share since entering the market in 2020 to
1.6% in 2025. IC substrate laminates carry significantly higher technical barriers than
conventional PCB laminates, with demanding qualification requirements and a

concentrated supplier base historically dominated by Japanese and Korean vendors
including Resonac, Mitsubishi Gas Chemical (MGC), and Doosan  etc. Ongoing substrate
material shortages have led several customers to qualify alternative suppliers, creating
an opportunity for EMC to penetrate programs supported by leveraging its longstanding
HDI and mSAP expertise as a natural pathway into substrate materials. We believe the
company is currently qualifying materials for AI ASIC-related substrate applications, with

initial shipments potentially commencing following customer approval. Looking
forward, EMC's first milestone is to reach 5% substrate market share, followed by 10%,
with both targets expected to be achieved within the next 12-24 months. We believe
successful execution against these milestones would establish substrates as a
meaningful incremental growth driver and further expand EMC's exposure to AI
infrastructure content growth.

### Page 6

EMC consists of three main business segments: Infrastructure, Mobile Devices, and
Automotive/Industrial.
1. Infrastructure (75-80% of Q326 revenue):
 AI Server (55-60% of Infrastructure)
 General Server (15-20% of Infrastructure)
 Switch (15-20% of Infrastructure)
 LEO satellite (5-10% of Infrastructure)
2. Mobile Devices (10-15% of Q326 revenue). Mobile device applications include
smartphones & tablets, notebooks and memory modules. These products utilize

HDI PCB designs to support device miniaturization, higher circuit density and high-
speed signal transmission.
3. Automotive/Industrial (5-10% of Q326 revenue). Automotive and industrial
applications include autonomous driving (ADAS), electric vehicles (power
conversion and charging), medical devices and industrial electronics, which require
high-reliability PCB materials for demanding operating environments.
Ongoing capacity expansions across Taiwan, Mainland China and Malaysia, and

a unique US production presence
EMC was founded in Taoyuan, Taiwan in 1992, where its headquarters and original CCL/
prepreg manufacturing base remain today. In 2005, EMC added its Hsinchu plant to
provide mass-lamination services for PCB customers requiring outsourced capacity. The
company has since built three major production sites in China, Kunshan in 1999,
Zhongshan in 2005, and Huangshi in 2019, serving Northern/Eastern, Southern and
Central China, respectively. In 2024, EMC established its Penang, Malaysia plant, its first

production base in Southeast Asia. In the US, EMC acquired Arlon EMD in 2020, making
it the only Asian CCL supplier with an active US production presence. In July 2026, EMC
raised planned investment in the site to US$69mn, with the expanded facility targeted
to commence operations from H226.
Management has laid out a substantial multi-year capacity expansion plan to capture
robust AI infrastructure demand and sustained tightness in high-end CCL. In Oct 2026,

the board approved an additional ~NT$98.6bn capex budget for phased factory
expansion across Taiwan, Mainland China and Malaysia, underscoring the scale of
EMC’s capacity build-out. Laminate capacity is expected to increase from 5.85mn
sheets/month in 2026E to 8.70mn in 2027E (+49% YoY), 10.45mn in 2028E (+20%
YoY), 11.95mn in 2029E (+14% YoY) and 15.25mn by 2030E (+28% YoY), implying a
~27% CAGR over 2026-30E.
EMC has nevertheless adjusted the phasing of certain expansion plans as it encountered

longer-than-expected expansion lead times in Taiwan. Most notably, the initial 300k
sheets/month Guanyin addition, previously targeted for Dec 2026, has been pushed
back by ~6 months due to labor/material constraints and longer government safety
inspections, while a further 600k sheets/month addition planned for 2028 has shifted to
2029. By contrast, capacity expansion at Kunshan, China can proceed more quickly.
Demand visibility remains robust, with signed LTAs exceeding 9.4mn sheets of

incremental capacity and supporting full utilization through 2028. Customers are
increasingly entering multi-year LTAs to secure high-end CCL supply, while management
continues to secure LTAs and verbal commitments beyond 2028 and sees customer
demand extending through 2030.

Shareholder structure:
Publicly listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in Nov 1998, EMC is chaired by Ting-Yu
Tung who directly  holds ~1.5% of outstanding shares. Related investment vehicles Yu
Chang Investment and Yu Sheng Investment together hold ~10.7%, bringing the
chairman/family-related holding to 12%. FINI ownership peaked at ~55% in 2017 amid
strong smartphone/HDI demand, before declining on smartphone weakness and Apple
SLP share loss. It bottomed at ~24% in 2024 amid concerns over Blackwell CCL share

loss to Korean competitors, but has since recovered to ~41% (vs Taiex's FINI holding
47%), supported by stronger-than-expected AI server/ASIC and high-end CCL demand.

Financial analysis
Q326 earnings beat on strong GM expansion and high-end mix. EMC delivered
record-high Q326 revenue of NT$60.9bn, up 29% QoQ and 142% YoY, driven by
strong infrastructure demand, price increases and favorable product mix. Gross margin
expanded 3.8ppts QoQ to 37.7%, with pricing and mix each contributing roughly half
of the improvement, while operating margin reached 30.7% and EPS rose to NT$40.47.
Infrastructure remained the core growth engine at 75-80% of revenue, with sales up

32% QoQ/176% YoY, while  full utilization underscored continued supply-demand
tightness amid strong high-end CCL demand.
Q426 to see continued pricing and mix momentum. For Q426E, EMC guided for
15-20% QoQ revenue growth and 38.0-39.5% GM, supported by continued price
increases and favorable product mix. By application, management expects infrastructure
revenue to grow ~20% QoQ, mobile to remain flattish, and automotive/industrial/
others to decline 5-10% QoQ, with the latter reflecting lower volumes as capacity is

reallocated toward high-speed CCL. We forecast Q426 revenue of NT$69.4bn (+14%
QoQ), GM to increase 0.5ppts to 38.2% and EPS of NT$47.03 (+16% QoQ). EMC plans
5-20% price increases across its portfolio in Q4, with pricing reflecting raw-material cost
increases; management also noted further upside to pricing if the supply-demand
imbalance persists.

Revenue and GM and EPS forecasts for 2027/2028E
We forecast EMC’s revenue to grow 80%/45% YoY in 2027/2028E, supported by
substantial capacity expansion, continued pricing and sustained high-end CCL demand.
Capacity is expected to increase ~49%/20% YoY in 2027/2028, with new capacity
coming online progressively and management expecting utilization to remain full

through 2028. EMC is accelerating investment to capture this demand, with capex rising
from NT$28bn in 2026 to NT$53bn in both 2027 and 2028, although FCF is expected to
remain negative for two years before turning positive in 2028-2029. Management
indicated signed LTAs already exceed 9.4mn sheets of incremental capacity, providing
strong visibility into the ramp, while substrate laminate represents additional upside,
with a new ASIC project expected to complete qualification by end-2026 and begin

shipments in 2027.
Pricing and product mix should provide additional growth on top of the capacity ramp.
EMC expects high-end CCL tightness to persist for the next two years, as extended
equipment lead times constrain the pace of new supply, while E-glass tightness could

### Page 9

persist until H227. Management sees further room for price increases if the supply-
demand imbalance persists. Product mix should provide another ASP and margin
tailwind, with M7+ expected to rise from 65-70% of Q326 sales to 70-80% in 2027. M9
began shipments in H226 and is expected to enter MP in 2027, while M10 qualification
is targeted for completion by end-2026, enabling initial shipments in H127 ahead of MP
in H227. With  typical generational upgrades carrying at least a 30% price increase, we

forecast GM to expand from 35.7% in 2026E to 40.5%/42.0% in 2027/2028E and
OPM to rise from 28.6% to 34.2%/36.2%, reflecting continued pricing, richer high-end
mix and operating leverage, driving EPS of NT$280.82/430.74, up 116%/53% YoY,
respectively. Our 2027/2028E EPS estimates are c.4%/4% below consensus, reflecting
more conservative utilization and pricing assumptions to account for potential downside
from slower AI/NVIDIA demand, while we remain positive on EMC’s technology

leadership and high-end product positioning.

Sensitivity analysis
Quantifying earnings downside  risks if AI spending slows down from 2028
The US Department of Commerce measures announced on 16 November seek to
prevent China AI-chip controls from being circumvented through third-country data

centers. The rules extend controls beyond the physical shipment of advanced chips to
who ultimately accesses the compute, while introducing licensing requirements for
advanced-chip exports, re-exports and transfers to Singapore, Malaysia, Indonesia and
Thailand, as well as Brazil, which BIS identifies as presenting higher-than-normal
diversion risk. Chips destined for US-headquartered companies or US-parented
subsidiaries are exempt from this country-level requirement, subject to reporting

conditions. The rules do not altogether prohibit ASEAN AI investment, but raise licensing
and compliance hurdles for parts of the data center ecosystem (LINK).  This raises
concerns on first-order impacts of likely to be delayed GPU deployment and slower data
center build-outs. While we believe AI data center build-out remains supported by cloud
computing power growth, enterprise AI and inference, we note it is constrained by data-
sovereignty and latency requirements, as well as power and land supply limits. We

### Page 10

believe the market continues to expect a strong 2027 but is now more cautious for
2028. We think some market expectations of a down year might be too conservative as
the UBS team has been noting that operating cash flow should support sustainable
capex for 2028. That being said, we still see some sensitivity around potential acts that
might cap hyperscalers from securing permitted sites and/or receiving power/water
permits.
Below we examine the potential downside risks into 2028 earnings if AI spending

materially slows down vs our current base case scenario. Our key assumptions and
findings are as follows:
 If capacity expansion plan falls short of expectations...: The company has
announced plans to expand CCL monthly capacity from 4.95mn sheets in 2025 to
10.45mn/15.25mn sheets by 2028F/2030F. Our current model assumes its CCL
monthly capacity of 10.08mn sheets in 2028E, and note that if capacity additions
were only 50%/30%/20%/10% executed compared to our base case scenario,

this will imply potential 2028E earnings downside of 25%/35%/39%/43% to our
current estimates.
 ...and pricing environment becomes less favourable: We currently expect to
see AI CCL content value increases driven by computing and networking/
interconnect demands; and extended equipment lead time and qualification
process along with increasing manufacturing challenges are also suggesting more
constrained upstream inputs. That being said, if pricing power starts to weaken on

AI spending slowdown, assuming pricing falls short of our current estimates by
5%/10%/15%/20%, on top of lowered capacity, this will imply potential earnings
downside of 32%/45%/53%/60% to our current 2028E estimates.

EMC’s 12-month forward P/E historically traded below 20x prior to 2023, before re-

rating with the onset of the AI cycle. The re-rating accelerated from 2025 through H126,
driven by 1) EMC’s leadership in M8 and expectations that this would extend into M9; 2)
growing exposure to hyperscaler ASIC programmes and expectations of regaining a
leading supplier position in NVIDIA’s Rubin platform; and 3) rising CCL content per
system as AI architectures adopt higher-specification materials and more complex board
designs. Expectations for CCL price increases and EMC’s technology and capacity

advantages provided further support in H126, taking its forward P/E to a historical peak
of 47x in April 2026. Since the AI cycle began, EMC has traded within a roughly 10-48x
forward P/E range, averaging around 20x.
The subsequent de-rating has been equally significant. EMC’s share price has fallen 56%
from its August peak, versus TAIEX down 21%, amid broader tech-sector selling, rising
concerns over slower AI spending, and uncertainty over whether PTFE adoption in

NVIDIA’s next-generation platforms could challenge EMC's high-end CCL positioning.
At NT$2,810 as of 20 November 2026, EMC trades at 10x/7x our 2027E/2028E EPS,
below its long-term historical median of 13.5x and well below its c.20x post-2023
average. Importantly, our 2027/2028E EPS estimates are already c.4%/4% below
consensus, partly reflecting a more conservative view amid rising AI demand concerns.
We believe the current valuation largely reflects these concerns, but gives insufficient

credit to EMC’s sustained technology leadership and earnings growth from continued
high-end material migration. Management expects M7+ to reach 70-80% of sales in
2027, versus 65-70% in Q326, while M9 is expected to enter mass production in 2027
and M10 could begin initial shipments in H127.
Our NT$5,000 price target is based on 12x 2028E P/E, at the mid to low end of EMC’s
7-20x historical valuation range. This compares with 8x for TUC versus its 7-17x

historical range and 10x for ITEQ versus its 9-19x historical range. We believe EMC’s
premium is warranted by its high-end material leadership, broader GPU and hyperscaler
ASIC exposure, and technology and capacity advantages. Our target multiple is also
above the 8x average for other CCL suppliers and 7x for PCB suppliers, while closer to
the 10x average for substrate suppliers as EMC expands its substrate CCL business.
EMC is trading at NT$2,810 (as of 20

Nov 2026)

growth, supported by continued AI demand, high-end material upgrades and capacity
expansion. We assume a gross margin of 42.0% and EPS of NT$430.7. We apply 12x

2028E P/E to derive our NT$5,000 base-case valuation.
DOWNSIDE (NT$1,650): We assume 2028E sales growth of 17.2%, driven by 22% CCL
growth, reflecting slower AI server/switch demand, delayed high-end material adoption
and an earlier-than-expected easing in CCL supply tightness. We assume a gross margin
of 36.8% and EPS of NT$253.8. We apply 7x 2028E P/E to derive our NT$1,650
downside valuation.

### Page 14

Elite Material UBS Research
Company Description
Company Description
Elite Material was founded in Taoyuan in 1992 as a conventional
FR-4 copper-clad thin-core laminate and prepreg manufacturer.
Over time, EMC expanded globally to become the world's largest
supplier of halogen-free high-speed CCLs, underpinned by its
leading technology expertise. In 2025, infrastructure
applications, including servers, switches, and LEO satellites,
accounted for 65-70% of revenue, followed by mobile devices

(20-25%), and automation, industrial and other applications (5-
10%).
Industry Outlook
The CCL industry outlook remains positive, supported by AI
server investment and upgrades to high-speed networking
infrastructure. Larger, higher-layer-count PCBs and tighter signal-
integrity requirements are increasing demand for high-
performance, ultra-low-loss laminates, supporting both material
content growth and a richer product mix. Tight supply of
specialty glass fabric and other inputs is supporting laminate

price increases, although rising material costs and supply
constraints remain risks to margins and shipment growth. These
trends should favour suppliers with advanced material
capabilities and reliable access to critical inputs.

Company Description
Elite Material was founded in Taoyuan in 1992 as a conventional FR-4 copper-clad thin-core
laminate and prepreg manufacturer. Over time, EMC expanded globally to become the
world's largest supplier of halogen-free high-speed CCLs, underpinned by its leading
technology expertise. In 2025, infrastructure applications, including servers, switches, and

LEO satellites, accounted for 65-70% of revenue, followed by mobile devices (20-25%), and
automation, industrial and other applications (5-10%).
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value EMC using a PE-based methodology.
Key risks include: 1) slower-than-expected high-end CCL adoption and AI infrastructure
deployments or delays in server upgrade cycles; 2) delays in capacity expansion or production
ramp-up; 3) stronger-than-expected competition and market-share losses in high-end CCL;

and 4) higher raw-material costs and weaker-than-expected cost pass-through to customers.

### Page 18

Quantitative Research Review
UBS Global Research publishes a quantitative assessment of its analysts' responses to certain questions about the likelihood of an occurrence
of a number of short term factors in a product known as the 'Quantitative Research Review'.  The views for this month can be found below.
Views contained in this assessment on a particular stock reflect only the views on those short term factors which are a different timeframe to

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over the next 12
months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is the one-year local
market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR) Stocks may be flagged
by the analyst to indicate that the stock's price target and/or rating may change in the near term, usually in response to an event that

may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have a 12-month investment horizon.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

大銀微簡介：大銀設立於 1997 年 4 月，為上銀的關係企業，產品線分為精密運動
及控制元件、微米與奈米級定位系統兩大類。元件類主要產品有線性馬達、 力矩
馬達、控制器、致動器等。定位平台為模組化產品，為客製化產品。公司與上銀共
同以「HIWIN」自有品牌行銷全球，服務地區涵蓋美國、德國、日本、瑞士、捷克、
以色列等。相關同業如台達、匯川等。 
3Q26 EPS 達 2.14 元，達新高水準：3Q26 營收 8.55 億元（+45.5%YoY），毛利
率達 42.4%，稅後淨利 2.57 億元（+257%YoY）， EPS 為 2.14 元，創下歷史新高；
累計前三季，EPS 為 4.06 元。第三季毛利率上升乃因 stage 出貨佔比增加以及規
模經濟發揮，本季 stage 佔比上升至 58%。 
訂單能見度 2-6 個月，產能持續擴充當中：現階段客戶訂單與出貨進度進展未有
變化，stage 訂單能見度 5-6 個月、交期 3 個月，元件訂單能見度 3 個月、交期
2-2.5 個月，stage 產能擴充第一階段已完成，反映在八月營收突破四億元，第二
階段預計明年 2-3 月完成，4 月可開始貢獻營收，目前已開始規畫第三階段擴廠；
元件明年投入自動化、半自動化機台來提升產能。公司預計今年整體資本支出 1.2-
1.5 億元。 
提升元件比重、stage 進入新應用領域為未來發展方向：現階段元件比重達 40%，
公司長期目標為 45%-50%，大銀微產品搭配上銀傳動元件來擴展市場，元件產品
潛在市場規模是 stage 的十倍，加以可以進行自動化或半自動化量產，規模擴大後
規模經濟效益更明顯。在 stage 產品，公司產品目前應用在非接觸式製程，未來將
進入精密鑽孔、塔式分選、Hybrid Bonding 等接觸式製程新領域；矽光子領域產
品已於 8 月開始出貨，產品應用在 insertion 3 光學引擎測試以及系統及測試，訂
單能見度很高，已可看到 2027 年中，使用公司產品可解決巡光耗時以及微小震動
可能導致光損，此可提升客戶光學檢測效率，與每小時產出量。 
毛利率預計可維持在 40%：在毛利率方面，公司預計第四季可維持在 40%，中長
期目標也在 40%上下，明年新產能開出與新產品、新領域的開拓，加上公司採用
自動化與半自動化提升效率、優化成本結構，毛利率可維持在 40%。 
財務預估： 2026 年 營 收 43.12 億元(+58.8 ％ YoY) ， 稅 後 淨 利 7.16 億元
（+196%YoY），EPS 為 5.98 元。2027 年營收 51.02 億元（+18%YoY），稅後
淨利 8.73 億元（+21%YoY），EPS 為 7.28 元。

矽力杰為電源管理晶片大廠：矽力杰成立於 02/2008，為電源管理 IC 設計公司，
產品線包括 DC/DC 轉換晶片、電池充電管理晶片、過流保護晶片、靜電防護裝置
與 LED 背光驅動晶片、能源監控管理晶片等。矽力杰 3Q26 產品終端應用主要區
分為五大領域，包括消費電子(如平板電腦、STB 與 TV)34%、工業應用(如 LED 照
明、伺服器與安防設備等)37%、資訊產品(如筆電)7%與網路通訊(如路由器、手機
等)7%、車載 15%。國際電源管理晶片設計同業與矽力杰產品類似者為 TI、Maxim、
ON Semi 與 ADI，台系類似的參與廠商則包括致新、茂達等。 
3Q26 營業費用較高：矽力杰公布 3Q26 營收 72.59 億元(+21%QoQ，+52%YoY)，
毛利率 47.58%，稅後獲利 9.09 億元(-42%QoQ，+34%YoY)，稅後 EPS 2.31 元，
財報不如預期，主要是營業費用規模偏高。矽力杰 3Q26 財報細項指標如下：(1) 
營收 72.59 億元(+21%QoQ，+52%YoY)，此係來自資料中心訂單加持、車載上
升(新款晶片推出與市占率提升)、消費電子季增 14%(即便市場需求疲弱，但因公
司新款晶片推出，故反而仍能向上)、資通訊產品下滑(因記憶體報價上漲，推高終
端售價而影響消費力道)。(2) 毛利率由 2Q26 的 47.03%微增至 47.42%，符合公
司原預期。(3) 營業費用 24.78 億元，季增達 19%，主要是銷售費用、資料中心應
用研發費用、3Q26 應屆畢業生招募人力增加。(4) 業外損失約 1100 萬元，相較
2Q26 業外收益 9.58 億元出現明顯差異，主要是前期有加特蘭微電子的再評價增
值利益產生。(5) 存貨 70.36 億元，季增 23%，存貨天數 150 天，達到歷史上緣位
置，公司表示係因封測供給吃緊，導致封測交期拉長(部分達到六個月)，因此 wafer bank
囤積在封測廠，導致庫存水位增加。

4Q26 營收持平到小增，略有不如預期：矽力杰預期(1) 4Q26 營收持平到小增(研
究部原預估 QoQ+5%~6%)，其中營運動能仍以資料中心為成長主軸，不過消費
電子市況相當疲弱(公司推測可能與客戶因應原物料與供應鏈不確定性，而提前出
貨，導致 4Q26 拉貨動能驟減)，包括手機與 TV 均顯弱勢。(2) 毛利率隨產品結構
變化、Gen4 製程平台滲透率提升至 20%，使得毛利率應能優於 3Q26 水準。
(3)4Q26~1H27 營業費用建議以 3Q26 的水準計算。研究部預估矽力杰 4Q26 營
收 74.29 億元(+2%QoQ，+38%YoY)，毛利率 47.84%，稅後獲利 10.5 億元
(+16%QoQ，+30%YoY)，稅後 EPS 2.67 元。 
2027 年營收成長性落於 YoY+20%~+30%上緣，反映成長力道延續：矽力杰維持
預期資料中心、車載與為主要成長推手(兩者 2027 年營收總計 YoY+>50%)，並給
予更明確的營收比重目標值：(1) 資料中心(包括主板/企業級 SSD/光模組)目前營
收比重 17%，2027 年底預計可達 25%。公司資料中心三大項目比重依序為光通
訊>SSD>MB，其中光通訊與 SSD 個別均為 mid-to-high single digit，MB 比重
雖較低，不過主板業務(包括控制晶片與 SPS)的新款晶片目前客戶均已處驗證中，
預計 2027 年將放量(公司表是北美客戶的 SPS 需求快於中國)。公司表示儘管近期
資料中心有諸多因素干擾，不過客戶針對明年訂單需求仍相當穩固。(2) 車載營收
比重可望由現階段 15%，攀升至 2027 年的 20%(原預估 15%~20%)，主要動能
來自於 content value 提升(由 200~200USD 往 270~300USD 邁進，係來自 MCU
與高電壓/大電流晶片出貨)、中國電動車的海外銷售攀升。(3) 公司期待隨 Gen4
製程平台 2027 年滲透率達 50%、Gen5 製程平台量產與產品結構持續往資料中
心、車載布局，對 2027 年毛利率>50%有信心。不過研究部認為矽力杰過往毛利
率容易受到存貨損失調整(因庫齡政策)影響，又製造成本上漲壓力延續，故雖預估
2027 年毛利率趨勢向上，但上升的幅度以較保守的態度推估。

維持買進：研究部預估矽力杰 2026 年營收 255.51 億元(+36%YoY)，毛利率
47.6%，稅後獲利 42.98 億元(+72%YoY)，稅後 EPS 10.92 元(原預估 11.95 元)，
並預估 2027 年營收 338.3 億元(+32%YoY)，毛利率 48.98%，稅後獲利 60.32 億
元(+40%YoY)，稅後 EPS 15.32 元。考量矽力杰資料中心與車載仍將持續帶動營
運拓展，研究部維持買進建議，目標價維持 644 元(42X 2027 年 EPS 評價)。

## Document 324: Cyber Glitches, Bootlegged Chips & Deflating China

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cyber Glitches, Bootlegged Chips & Deflating China.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Cyber Glitches, Bootlegged Chips & Deflating China
Date:    8 hours ago
Cyber Glitches, Bootlegged Chips & Deflating China

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Cyber Glitches, Bootlegged
Chips & Deflating China
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 16 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Tonik / Unsplash

I. Cyber Glitches & Chip Controls
"Once is happenstance. Twice is coincidence. Three times is enemy
action." That's according to Ian Fleming’s Goldfinger (1959)

### Page 2

What, then, should we make of the recent string of technology
disruptions? In October, hackers tampered with freezers at roughly 700
French supermarkets, spoiling food and triggering fires. Last Thursday,
Microsoft suffered a 10-hour outage affecting Outlook and Teams. On
Friday, the Federal Aviation Administration (FAA) imposed the longest
nationwide flight ground stop since 9/11. Then, today, customers at major
US banks, including JPMorgan Chase, reported problems accessing their

accounts, while a computer failure temporarily grounded United Airlines
flights.
France blamed Iran's Islamic Revolutionary Guard Corps for the
supermarket attacks, although Tehran denies responsibility. The other
disruptions appear to have been technical failures, including software
bugs at Microsoft and JPMorgan Chase and a malfunctioning router at the
FAA. United's outage was resolved within 90 minutes. No evidence links
these incidents, but their timing is curious, to say the least.

Regulatory loopholes might also be considered glitches, and one may
finally be getting fixed. On Monday, the US Commerce Department
announced tighter restrictions to prevent Chinese companies from
accessing American AI chips and computing power through overseas data
centers, particularly in Southeast Asia. The measures follow scrutiny of
Nvidia's ties to Singapore-based Megaspeed, which has been accused of
circumventing US export controls.
The announcement added to the semiconductor stock selloff as investors

worried about lost chip sales and slower overseas data center investment.
The Philadelphia Semiconductor Index (SOX) plunged 5.3% today, while
the iShares Semiconductor ETF (SOXX) fell 5.4%. Nvidia declined 4.1%,
AMD 5.6%, and Micron more than 7%. The Nasdaq Composite dropped
2.5%, remaining below its 200-day moving average (charts).

### Page 3

Investors are particularly worried about Nvidia. How much of its revenue
could tighter export controls put at risk? Will CEO Jensen Huang's
departure unravel Nvidia's circular financing strategy and the broader AI
investment boom? Tomorrow's earnings call, led by interim CEO Colette
Kress, could provide crucial answers.
In any event, money appears to be rotating out of technology and into the
broader market. The S&P 500 equal-weight stock index has outperformed

its market-weight counterpart in recent weeks (chart). The former remains
near its recent record high, while the latter is down 5.1% from its record
high on October 5. The Nasdaq is 11.2% below its October 5 record high.
SOXX is down 35.3% from its June 22 record high. The market is holding
up remarkably well given the shellacking of the semiconductor stocks.

### Page 4

II. China Is Weakening
China's economy continues to lose steam, with domestic demand
particularly weak. Consumers are depressed. Deflation is mounting
because of excess production. Here’s more:
(1) Retail sales & production. Retail sales rose just 0.4% y/y in October.
Inflation-adjusted retail sales fell 0.4%, highlighting persistent weakness in
domestic demand. Both measures are hovering around zero, a sharp
contrast to the double-digit growth rates that prevailed before the

pandemic.
Chinese consumers remain deeply pessimistic, with the consumer
confidence index far below its historical average (chart). They've been hit

### Page 5

by a huge negative wealth effect in the property market. They've also
been losing money in the stock and gold markets this year.
Industrial production rose 4.8% y/y in October despite declining real retail
sales, highlighting the imbalance between China's manufacturing output
and domestic consumption that pushes Chinese producers to dump more
goods onto global markets (chart).
Automobile production remains near record highs at an annualized 27.9
million vehicles, despite weak domestic demand (chart). Total domestic

passenger vehicle retail sales over the trailing 12-month period through
October came to roughly 19.5 million units, down about 20% y/y. The glut
has been exported.

### Page 6

(2) Inflation. China's inflation data further confirm weak domestic demand
and excess production. Headline CPI rose just 0.7% y/y in October, while
prices excluding food and energy declined 0.4% y/y (chart).
The prolonged property downturn is a major drag on Chinese consumers.
New house prices across 70 cities fell 2.4% y/y in October (chart). New
house prices have been falling since 2022.

### Page 7

Financial markets reflect these challenges. Chinese real estate stocks
remain deeply depressed, with the Shenzhen Real Estate Index near
levels last seen during the global financial crisis (chart). The CSI 300 has
been volatile and trendless for years.
(3) Trade. China's manufacturing overcapacity is increasingly spilling into
global markets. Merchandise exports have surged to a record $4.2 trillion
annualized, far outpacing imports of $3.1 trillion (chart).

### Page 8

As a result, China's trade surplus reached a record $1.27 trillion over the
past 12 months (chart).
China's crude oil imports, adjusted for Brent prices, are flattening after
decades of growth (chart). That's largely because more Chinese are
driving electric vehicles. This development was weighing on global oil
prices until the war in the Middle East boosted them this year.

### Page 9

China's exporters are increasingly looking beyond the US, with shipments
to Europe surging to nearly $890 billion over the past year, almost twice
the $463 billion shipped to America (chart).
Exports to Vietnam have also soared, to a record $250 billion, alongside
strong growth in shipments to South Korea and India (chart).

### Page 10

(4) Money & credit. China's credit boom has left its banking system with
enormous outstanding loans of $41.9 trillion, almost three times the $14.2
trillion outstanding at US banks (chart).
Bank loan growth continues to slow, with bank loans and M2 growth rates
in clear downward trends (chart).

### Page 11

Weak bank credit growth reflects the broader economic malaise, even as
Chinese interest rates remain exceptionally low (chart).
The People's Bank of China continues accumulating gold, reflecting its
efforts to diversify official reserves (chart).

### Page 12

clause.
Yardeni QuickTakes © 2026 – Unsubscribe

## Document 355: Housing sentiment sinks but buying intentions positive (2026-11-24)

Date: 2026-11-24

### Page 1

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
WESTPAC HOUSING
SENTIMENT BULLETIN
Latest insights on the Australian consumer
17 November 2026
Key points
• Westpac Housing Sentiment Index
down 2.2% to 83.2.
• Index measures and new questions
add timely gauges to housing cycle.
• Housing-related sentiment remains

very weak but a touch above 2022
low.
• Latest monthly fall centred on
a sharp downgrade in price
expectations.
• State measures show larger
deterioration in WA, milder read in
Victoria.
• Nearly 13% of Australians planning
to buy a home next year, matching
2025 high.
• Just 8.2% plan to sell a home,
lowest since 2024.

Housing Sentiment Index
“The Housing Sentiment Index
declined 2.2% to 83.2 in November
from 85.3 in October.”

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
All state Housing Sentiment Indexes are at weak levels.
However, November saw a bigger decline in Western
Australia (–4.8%mth) which now has the weakest housing-
related sentiment index across the major states at just 80.9.
Queensland and South Australia moved in line with the

national index. Moves were notably milder in New South
Wales (–1.7%) and especially Victoria (–0.7%) where the state
index, at 86.6, is the least pessimistic of the major states.
The sub-group detail showed a more pronounced weakening in
housing-related sentiment amongst high income earners and
consumers aged over 55 with sentiment holding about steady
amongst low-income earners, albeit at weaker overall levels.
Our November consumer sentiment survey also included

two new regular questions on consumers’ property buying
and selling intentions over the next twelve months. These
questions will continue to be run every month.
Despite the broader pessimism around housing, a relatively
high number of consumers are planning to buy in 2027.
Responses show 12.8% of consumers plan to buy a new
home over the next year. Comparable figures from Westpac’s
Home Ownership survey going back to 2019 suggest this is a
relatively firm result, up slightly since the middle of the year,

on par with the start of 2025 and above the levels reported
during the COVID pandemic in 2020-21. The result suggests
there is significant ‘latent demand’ for home purchases in
Australia, and that many buyers are just waiting for the right
opportunity. The state detail shows intentions are particularly
high in Queensland where one in five consumers is reportedly
planning to buy in 2027.
Interestingly, the positive intentions are not just around home

purchase with 11.2% of consumers reporting that they plan
to buy an investment property over the next year. Again,
comparable figures from Westpac’s Home Ownership survey
show this is up a tick compared to May and only marginally
below the 11.5% reporting intentions to buy investment
properties back in January 2025.
BULLETIN
The Westpac Housing Sentiment Index fell 2.2% to 83.2 in
November from 85.3 in October.
The Index is a composite based on housing-related sentiment

measures from the Westpac-Melbourne Institute Consumer
Sentiment survey. It already appears regularly in Westpac’s
Housing Pulse reports but will now be published on a
monthly basis. The Index has proven to be an extremely useful
predictor of turning points in housing markets, both nationally
and across states. Westpac is also introducing two new
survey questions on consumers property buying and selling
intentions that will be run every month.
The November update shows housing-related sentiment

remains remain deeply pessimistic. At 85.3, the latest Index
read is in the bottom 3% of observations since 2005, when all
components first became available. That said, it is still above the
81.4 low in late 2022 and the 78.6 registered when the COVID
pandemic hit in 2020. Housing-related sentiment was markedly
weaker during the slump in the late 1980s/early 1990s.
Component-wise, the November sentiment decline centred on
a sharp downgrade to price expectations. As detailed below,

the Westpac Housing Sentiment Index composite is based
on four sub-indexes: one tracking assessments of ‘time to buy
a dwelling’; one tracking ‘consumer house price expectations’;
one tracking consumer expectations for unemployment and a
measure of risk aversion based on consumer assessments of
the ‘wisest place for savings’.
Consumer price expectations took a big step down in
November. The Westpac–Melbourne Institute Index of
House Price Expectations recorded a steep 13.4% drop

to 99.6, marking the second biggest monthly deterioration
since the COVID period and the first net negative (sub-100)
read since November 2022. The detailed responses show
consumers are now evenly split on the outlook, just over 38%
expecting more declines, 19% expecting no change and 38%
expecting a rise (5% reported “don’t know).
At least some consumers are starting to see lower prices as
a potential buying opportunity. The ‘time to buy a dwelling’

index rose 1.3% to 89.6 in November, to be up 1.3% on a year
ago as well but still at a very weak level overall, well below
the long run average of 114.
The wider economic backdrop was also less conducive for
housing-related sentiment. Consumers continue to report
a gradual rise in job-loss fears and risk aversion remains
elevated near historic highs. Both factors are likely to be
deterring prospective home buyers.
Housing sentiment sinks but buying intentions positive

“Despite the broader pessimism
around housing, a relatively high
number of consumers are planning
to buy ...”
Matthew Hassan
Head of Australian Macro-Forecasting

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Conversely, just 8.2% of Australians are planning to sell a home 
over the next year. Comparable figures from Westpac’s Home 
Ownership survey suggest this is down significantly from 10.3% 
back in May and near the 7.7% low recorded at the start of 2024. 
On investment properties, 6.7% of all those surveyed reported 
plans to sell. However, only 23% of consumers reported 
owning an investment property – as a share of this sub-
group, the proportion planning to sell is just over 26%. While 
it is unclear whether this is relatively high (no comparable 
measures are available from Westpac’s Home Ownership 
survey), a third of this same sub-group reported an intention to 
buy an investment property over the next year, indicating that 
amongst investment property owners prospective buyers still 
outnumber prospective sellers. 
Overall, the consumer views on housing show a clear tension. 
On the one side, high interest rates, a continued slide in prices 
and unease about a riskier outlook for jobs and the wider 
economy are weighing heavily on housing-related sentiment. 
But against this, relatively few are planning to sell and there 
is a relatively high proportion of consumers looking to buy. 
This suggests that conditions could recover quite quickly if and 
when the macro-economic backdrop becomes less threatening 
and the market starts to stabilise.

The survey is conducted by OZINFO & DYNATA. Respondents are selected at random. This latest survey is based on 1200 adults aged 18 years and over, across Australia. It was conducted in the week from 
2 November to 6 November.  The data have been weighted to reflect Australia’s population distribution. Copyright at all times remains with the Melbourne Institute of Applied Economic and Social Research.
About the Westpac Housing Sentiment Index 
The Westpac Housing Sentiment Index is a composite 
measure based on a weighted combination of four indexes 
from the Westpac–Melbourne Institute Consumer 
Sentiment survey. Two of these are ‘primary’ components 
with a higher weight that relate directly to consumer 
perceptions of housing market conditions: the Westpac–
Melbourne Institute ‘time to buy a dwelling’ index and the 
Westpac–Melbourne Institute House Price Expectations 
Index. The remaining ‘supplementary’ components, with 
lower weights, relate to consumer assessments of job 
security – the Westpac–Melbourne Institute Unemployment 
Expectations Index – and risk appetite – the Westpac Risk 
Aversion Index.
Each of these components is seasonally adjusted, converted 
to a consistent base and combined using fixed weights 
determined by historical regression analysis. Note that the 
house price expectations component is only available from 
2009 on – a re-weighted composite based on the remaining 
measures is used for earlier periods.  
The resulting composite measures provide significant insight 
into housing market conditions both nationally and at the 
individual state level. The national index has over 50yrs of 
history and a clear lead indicator relationship with a variety 
of housing market metrics. The index is particularly good at 
picking turning points in housing market turnover – correctly 
anticipating every major upswing and downturn since 1980 
with a lead of around three months (four once the timeliness 
of sentiment updates is included).

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
TODAY’S INSIGHTS
Financial Markets
•  Share markets rallied across much of the globe. In the
US, reports of an upgraded outlook for CoreWeave, an
oversubscribed IPO for Anthropic and a stabilisation in
Nvidia supported renewed optimism around AI which

buoyed tech stocks, seeing the S&P 500 (+1.4%) and
Nasdaq (+1.7%) both rally, though the smaller gain for the
Dow Jones (+0.5%) shows the rally was more narrowly
based.
•  Across the pond, the Euro Stoxx 50 continued to build on
recent momentum with another solid gain (+1.0%), while
London’s FTSE 100 bounced back from a modest decline
yesterday (+0.8%). Stocks were up across most of Asia,
including Tokyo (+1.3%), Shanghai (+0.4%) and Seoul
(+2.6%), with only the Hang Seng (–1.0%) bucking the

wider trend. The ASX 200 rose 0.3% yesterday, but weaker
trading in futures overnight points to a mixed open this
morning.
•  Cash trading in bond markets were closed in the US for
Veterans Day, but trading in futures points to a continued
rise in yields following hotter-than-expected inflation
data received earlier in the week, which all but cemented
a December FOMC rate hike. Beyond December, markets
are also pricing in another two rate hikes over the course of

2027.
• Yields were little changed elsewhere, with the 10Y Bund
and Gilt steady on the day. The 10Y JGB meanwhile
continued to sell-off, with yields jumping 10bps to 2.80%
having effectively unwound the rally over late last week.
ACGBs were also weaker during local trading, seeing yields
rise 7bps on the 3Y and 6bps on the 10Y, following the
sharp rise in global yields the day prior post US CPI. Swaps
markets point to no hikes nor cuts from the RBA through to

end-2027 at present.
•  The USD received a solid bid overnight, buoyed by sharp
inflows into AI and tech stocks that improved risk sentiment.
The DXY finished 0.7% higher at around 102.9, the
strongest since April 2025. That move has pushed the Euro
down 0.6% back to a USD1.10 handle, the weakest since
May 2025, while the Yen slipped 0.7% to JPY159.9, the
weakest since the before the early-September rally. While
moves in the AUD was comparatively small overnight,

slipping 0.2% to around the USD0.6850 mark, that move
builds on recent downward momentum and has the currency
delicately positioned near 2026’s lows.
•  Oil prices rose slightly overnight, stabilising somewhat after
recent declines amid ongoing uncertainty around the future
of the US-Iran War, as President Trump downplays the
possibility of further strikes on Iran all while negotiations
show little progress. Brent rose 1.0% to US$92.4/bbl. The
rebound in risk sentiment buoyed metals, with LME copper

up 1.0% to US$13,817/mt while LME aluminium jumped
1.9% to US$3,068/mt. Gold prices meanwhile dipped 1.2%,
falling back below the US$4,000/oz mark, building on recent
momentum post US CPI which saw real yields jump higher
International Data
There was no major data out yesterday.
Domestic Data
In Australia, the value of dwelling finance approvals surprised
materially to the downside in Q3, the 15.5% decline marking
the largest quarterly decline on records going back to 1991.

The decline looks to be mostly volume-driven, with the number
of loans falling sharply compared to the average size of loans.
Across segments, investor lending values dropped by nearly a
third (see here).

第⼀個驗證點。

CoWoS 份額相對原本路徑 2027 年少 2、2028 年少 4 個百分點
管理層 8 ⽉表⽰明年約四分之⼀業務需輝達資產負債表⽀撐；新任管理層

若縮減新增融資，部分需求轉向 AMD 與 ASIC、部分隨之消失。⿈仁勳拍
板之產能預訂與規格升級亦可能放慢，影響最快在 2028 年顯現。CoWoS
總量相對原本路徑僅約 −0.6%、−0.8%。
Google TPU 與 AMD 最直接受益，份額移轉在事件前已開始

份額不會流向中國；韓國之⾓⾊在⿈仁勳任內已擴⼤，美國是⽣

10 ⽉ 29 ⽇（美東時間，下同）媒體報導美國情報評估，⼀名晶⽚買家之主管把資訊傳回北京、並與⿈仁勳關係密切；11

員⼯承認對婚姻不忠。11 ⽉ 10 ⽇獨⽴董事委任前紐約南區代理聯邦檢察官主持調查；同⽇有媒體報導相關公司網絡協助
⾄少 75 億美元之輝達晶⽚流⼊中國，此數字未經其他媒體證實，輝達先前亦稱相關轉運指控已查明不實。11 ⽉ 11 ⽇兩黨

⽇上午（台北時間 21:52）以 8-K 公告。

治理結構同步調整：⾸度設⽴董事⻑，暫代執⾏⻑兼任財務⻑
⿈仁勳辭去總裁、執⾏⻑與董事，公司發⾔⼈表⽰其⽇後不以任何形式參與輝達。財務⻑ Colette Kress（2013 年加⼊，
曾任 Cisco 與 Microsoft 事業部財務主管）暫代執⾏⻑並續任財務⻑；1993 年起擔任董事、曾任 Synopsys 執⾏⻑之
Harvey C. Jones 出任輝達⾸任董事⻑（輝達過去不設董事⻑）。董事會將考慮內外部⼈選並委請外部公司協助，未公布時
程。第三季財報維持 11 ⽉ 17 ⽇公布；據眾議院外交委員會主席表⽰，⿈仁勳同意於感恩節前赴國會作證。

2027 年以前
2027 年之出貨⼤致鎖定：產品已量產、產能已預付、承諾已簽訂
Vera Rubin 已量產出貨，主要出貨量在 2027 年；Rubin Ultra 以前之規格已定案
輝達 8 ⽉表⽰ Vera Rubin 於當⽉開始量產出貨，管理層預估約占第三季資料中⼼營收兩成；鴻海表⽰ Vera Rubin 第四季
放量、為明年主⼒，廣達表⽰第四季⼩量、2027 年第⼀季放量。管理層 8 ⽉預估 2028 會計年度（約當 2027 年 2 ⽉⾄
2028 年 1 ⽉）營收成⻑約七成，屬供給受限之數字；3 ⽉ GTC 時稱 Blackwell 與 Vera Rubin ⾄ 2027 年之訂單能⾒度⾄少

1 兆美元。據供應鏈訪查，Rubin Ultra 與 Feynman 之開發已進⼊執⾏階段；新任管理層第⼀個能實質調整的，是
Feynman 世代之規格、代⼯與封裝分配。
供應商受合約保護：2,790 億美元之採購與產能承諾，加上客⼾之預付款
輝達 7 ⽉底之季報揭露表外之供應與產能採購承諾 2,790 億美元（前⼀季 1,190 億美元），主要為記憶體與製造設施，台積
電、HBM 廠與主要組裝廠 2027 年之產能多已被預訂。下游⽅⾯，廣達 11 ⽉ 11 ⽇法說會表⽰，迄今未⾒客⼾調整訂單，
客⼾若延後拉貨需負擔相關利息與費⽤；以 CoreWeave 為例，其積壓訂單約 1,325 億美元，未來三個⽉另有約 90 億美元
之客⼾預付款與押⾦，綁定的是機房與晶⽚。
近期之⼲擾來⾃轉換期與出⼝管制，⽽⾮需求
據供應鏈訪查，第四季 VR200、Trainium 3 與 TPU v8 之新平台轉換均因零組件與組裝良率⽽遞延，屬時程問題。較需留
意的是出⼝管制：據報美國商務部正敲定新規，把⾺來西亞、新加坡等 7 國納⼊較嚴格之限制，涵蓋 Blackwell 以上晶⽚

及遠端使⽤；和碩 11 ⽉ 12 ⽇法說會表⽰此類限制並⾮新規，已納⼊法務審核。國會亦把晶⽚追蹤（Chip Security Act）
與出⼝許可審查（AI Overwatch Act）列為國防授權法之候選條⽂。本次事件涉及外國情報與晶⽚流向之指控，調查結果
出爐前，管制收緊之機率已上升，台灣組裝廠出貨東南亞機房之合規成本將提⾼。
接班情境
三種接班路徑對產品節奏、供應商議價與客⼾融資之影響不同；ToAlpha 以內部技術主
管接任為基本情境
董事會之⾸要任務是穩定客⼾與⼯程團隊，內部⼈接任之機率最⾼
據報導與預測市場，內部⼈選以 CUDA 創始⼈、現負責⼤型雲端與 HPC 業務之 Ian Buck 呼聲最⾼，其次為暫代之
Kress；技術⻑ Michael Kagan、⾸席科學家 Bill Dally 與 GPU ⼯程主管 Jonah Alben 亦被提及。外部⼈選包括曾任職輝
3 / 12

### Page 4

達七年之 Arm 執⾏⻑ Rene Haas；Apple 硬體主管 Johny Srouji 據報亦在考慮之列，美國總統則據報公開推薦前 Apple
執⾏⻑ Tim Cook。全球業務主管 Jay Puri 已於 6 ⽉宣布退休。輝達沒有公開之接班計畫、約 60 位主管直接向⿈仁勳報
告；正式⼈選產⽣前由 Kress 暫代，市場預期約⼀年。ToAlpha 以內部技術主管接任為基本情境（45%），營運財務型（含
Kress 續任或外部營運型）30%，外部產業執⾏⻑ 25%。
無論何⼈接任，新增融資與規格升級之積極程度都可能低於⿈仁勳時代
輝達 7 ⽉底持有⾮上市股權約 512 億美元、上市股權約 428 億美元，另有最⾼ 1,085 億美元之保證與 250 億美元之股權投
資承諾；運算承諾⽅⾯，OpenAI 規劃約 12GW 之輝達運算、Anthropic 最多 1GW。已簽承諾會延續，但新增部分之審核
標準可能提⾼：據報 Kress 曾反對為 OpenAI 提供最⾼ 1,050 億美元之擔保；市場亦期待新任管理層提⾼現⾦回饋、回應

「循環融資」之疑慮。規格升級⽅⾯，據供應鏈訪查，⿈仁勳之產品策略對供應鏈有「產能預訂」與「規格升級」兩項激
進影響，我們認為這兩項也是輝達供應鏈過去享有較⾼評價之重要原因。新任管理層若偏重成本與執⾏穩定，800V
HVDC、共同封裝光學、M9 等級銅箔基板等升級之導⼊可能放慢，供應鏈成本管理亦會更嚴格。

產品路線
Feynman 以前之路線圖已進⼊執⾏，新任管理層可調整之處在決策速度與規格升級
路線圖本⾝未變，但 2026 年已出現取捨：Rubin CPX 擱置、Kyber 機櫃傳延⾄ 2028 年
Rubin 為兩顆光罩極限尺⼨晶粒、每顆搭載 288GB HBM4 之設計[5]，量產爬坡已受 HBM4 規格與良率牽制。輝達今年並
把原訂 2026 年底之 Rubin CPX 移出路線圖，改以 Groq 授權技術之 LPX 推論機櫃處理解碼；產業研究機構 7 ⽉指出
Kyber 機櫃因 PCB 中背板之可製造性延⾄ 2028 年，輝達回應路線圖不變。這顯⽰即使在⿈仁勳任內，年更節奏已觸及供

應鏈之物理極限。我們認為新任管理層較可能之調整，是把年更改為「⼤世代兩年、中間做強化版」，並集中資源於推論
（LPX、Vera CPU）與網路（Spectrum-X、共同封裝光學）。
開放策略可能加速：NVLink Fusion 讓 ASIC 客⼾也採購輝達元件
NVLink Fusion 已納⼊ AWS Trainium4、Marvell 客製 XPU 與 Intel 客製 x86 CPU，輝達 3 ⽉並⼊股 Marvell 20 億美元。
對新任管理層⽽⾔，與其與 ASIC 正⾯競爭，不如在 ASIC 機櫃內銷售 CPU、網卡、交換器與 NVLink；輝達之營收來源因
此分散，但 GPU 本⾝之份額讓渡也會更快。對台灣供應鏈⽽⾔，交換器、網卡相關供應商不必選邊。
